# 分析会计、预算与经营分析

维护分析计划、分析账户、适用规则、分摊模型、分析分录和分录行分析分摊，检查分析账户余额及发票使用，管理预算生命周期、预算行、报表预算定义和期间总额，并查询发票及会计分录行分析汇总。分析币种与分组口径按原生报告合同读取；销售采购订单统计见历史订单场景。

[回到总说明书](../../CLI_V4_MANUAL.md) · [新会话使用指南](../USAGE_GUIDE.md)

<a id="cap-analytic-account-archive"></a>

## analytic.account.archive — 停用分析账户

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.plan；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.account:write, account.analytic.plan:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.account.archive.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.account.archive.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.account.archive --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "analytic.account.archive:1" --confirm "analytic.account.archive"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "analytic_account_id": 1
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_analytic_account_archive
- `verify`：same_transaction_inactive_state_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：target_active_state_recheck_without_operation_store
- `reverse`：analytic.account.restore

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Focused unit tests cover the closed identifier contract, company scope, target-state replay, registry metadata, and schemas.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_cli.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded transactional smoke verifies execution, immediate replay, and rollback in both dedicated isolated database aliases.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_write_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples are deferred until the capability library reaches target coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing is outside this capability batch.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-account-balance-inspect"></a>

## analytic.account.balance.inspect — 查看指定期间原生分析借贷和余额

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_account_balance_inspect`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability depends on configured database, company and business-user ACLs.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.plan, account.analytic.line, res.currency；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.line:read, res.currency:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.account.balance.inspect.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.account.balance.inspect.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.account.balance.inspect --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "analytic_account_id": 1,
    "date_from": "2026-10-01",
    "date_to": "2026-10-31"
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：request_schema_validation
- `execute`：fixed_native_analytic_compute_or_rule_resolution
- `verify`：closed_typed_result_and_query_scope_validation
- `idempotency`：read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Closed request normalization and schemas, public CLI confirmation and deterministic keys, typed company/query/result binding, malformed input and unavailable/result identity rejection, and no fabricated delete replay. Actual native methods and ACL/constraint behavior are verified by the shared integration workflow, not by these port-level unit fixtures.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_processing_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；One shared rollback-only public CLI/real-ORM workflow passed both isolated aliases as uid 5/su=False/company 1: eight new IDs, ten setup IDs and sixteen immediate replays per alias. Native date-scoped debit/credit/balance is exact, archived/shared reads work, foreign-company shared-account lines are excluded and dated computation does not pollute all-time cache. Native posted usage counts distinct move IDs: two matching lines on one customer invoice count as one invoice, plus one vendor bill; drafts contribute zero, reflecting analytic.mixin SELECT DISTINCT move_id/account_id. Native copies preserve code/partner/active/plan/company, do not copy lines, retain the source, support serial replay/profile conflict/ambiguous-name rejection and recreate deleted targets with fresh archived identities. Native analytic-line account FK deletion denial is checked directly and preserves source/lines; unused owned accounts delete with no missing-target replay. Native applicability prefix/product-category scoring and distribution prefix/partner/tags/product/category precedence use actual references; deleting company-owned rules/models changes resolution, including native shared fallback. Distribution resolution binds audit metadata to its company singleton, not a fictitious distribution-model ID. All four writes reject shared/foreign targets; missing references are denied. Posted entries and existing plans are unchanged. Fresh-cursor synthetic data and temporary manager/analytic group rollback verified. Existing project root used, no plan mutation or dynamic DDL; no multi-root fixture coverage or historical dated FX claim. No business DB, addon/source, service or external payment changes.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_processing_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-account-create"></a>

## analytic.account.create — 创建分析账户

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`odoo_native_analytic_account_idempotency_field_unavailable` — The command is implemented, but Odoo has no native unique analytic-account idempotency field; V4 uses a visible deterministic name suffix and cannot promise concurrent exactly-once creation.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.plan, res.partner；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：res.partner:read, account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.account:create。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.account.create.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.account.create.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.account.create --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "doc-example-operation-001" --confirm "analytic.account.create"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json

  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "name": "Example",
    "plan_id": 1
  }
}
```

<a id="cap-analytic-account-delete"></a>

## analytic.account.delete — 原生删除公司分析账户

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability depends on configured database, company and business-user ACLs.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：account.analytic.account, res.company；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, mail；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.account:unlink, res.company:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.account.delete.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.account.delete.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式


### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：native_copy_or_unlink_as_business_user_for_company_owned_targets
- `verify`：same_savepoint_identity_and_native_configuration_reread
- `idempotency`：serial_company_name_profile_replay_for_copy_and_no_missing_delete_replay
- `reverse`：backup_subject_to_native_acl_and_constraints

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Closed request normalization and schemas, public CLI confirmation and deterministic keys, typed company/query/result binding, malformed input and unavailable/result identity rejection, and no fabricated delete replay. Actual native methods and ACL/constraint behavior are verified by the shared integration workflow, not by these port-level unit fixtures.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_processing_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；One shared rollback-only public CLI/real-ORM workflow passed both isolated aliases as uid 5/su=False/company 1: eight new IDs, ten setup IDs and sixteen immediate replays per alias. Native date-scoped debit/credit/balance is exact, archived/shared reads work, foreign-company shared-account lines are excluded and dated computation does not pollute all-time cache. Native posted usage counts distinct move IDs: two matching lines on one customer invoice count as one invoice, plus one vendor bill; drafts contribute zero, reflecting analytic.mixin SELECT DISTINCT move_id/account_id. Native copies preserve code/partner/active/plan/company, do not copy lines, retain the source, support serial replay/profile conflict/ambiguous-name rejection and recreate deleted targets with fresh archived identities. Native analytic-line account FK deletion denial is checked directly and preserves source/lines; unused owned accounts delete with no missing-target replay. Native applicability prefix/product-category scoring and distribution prefix/partner/tags/product/category precedence use actual references; deleting company-owned rules/models changes resolution, including native shared fallback. Distribution resolution binds audit metadata to its company singleton, not a fictitious distribution-model ID. All four writes reject shared/foreign targets; missing references are denied. Posted entries and existing plans are unchanged. Fresh-cursor synthetic data and temporary manager/analytic group rollback verified. Existing project root used, no plan mutation or dynamic DDL; no multi-root fixture coverage or historical dated FX claim. No business DB, addon/source, service or external payment changes.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_processing_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-account-duplicate"></a>

## analytic.account.duplicate — 原生复制公司分析账户

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`concurrent_idempotency_limit` — Native analytic names are not unique: company/name/profile matching supports serial replay, not concurrent exactly-once or original-copy provenance.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：account.analytic.account, account.analytic.plan, res.company, res.partner；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, mail；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：account.analytic.account:create, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.plan:read, res.company:read, res.partner:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.account.duplicate.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.account.duplicate.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.account.duplicate --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "analytic.account.duplicate:1:575a4fc72f8ff54574c4483a561b3851" --confirm "analytic.account.duplicate"
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：native_copy_or_unlink_as_business_user_for_company_owned_targets
- `verify`：same_savepoint_identity_and_native_configuration_reread
- `idempotency`：serial_company_name_profile_replay_for_copy_and_no_missing_delete_replay
- `reverse`：backup_subject_to_native_acl_and_constraints

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Closed request normalization and schemas, public CLI confirmation and deterministic keys, typed company/query/result binding, malformed input and unavailable/result identity rejection, and no fabricated delete replay. Actual native methods and ACL/constraint behavior are verified by the shared integration workflow, not by these port-level unit fixtures.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_processing_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；One shared rollback-only public CLI/real-ORM workflow passed both isolated aliases as uid 5/su=False/company 1: eight new IDs, ten setup IDs and sixteen immediate replays per alias. Native date-scoped debit/credit/balance is exact, archived/shared reads work, foreign-company shared-account lines are excluded and dated computation does not pollute all-time cache. Native posted usage counts distinct move IDs: two matching lines on one customer invoice count as one invoice, plus one vendor bill; drafts contribute zero, reflecting analytic.mixin SELECT DISTINCT move_id/account_id. Native copies preserve code/partner/active/plan/company, do not copy lines, retain the source, support serial replay/profile conflict/ambiguous-name rejection and recreate deleted targets with fresh archived identities. Native analytic-line account FK deletion denial is checked directly and preserves source/lines; unused owned accounts delete with no missing-target replay. Native applicability prefix/product-category scoring and distribution prefix/partner/tags/product/category precedence use actual references; deleting company-owned rules/models changes resolution, including native shared fallback. Distribution resolution binds audit metadata to its company singleton, not a fictitious distribution-model ID. All four writes reject shared/foreign targets; missing references are denied. Posted entries and existing plans are unchanged. Fresh-cursor synthetic data and temporary manager/analytic group rollback verified. Existing project root used, no plan mutation or dynamic DDL; no multi-root fixture coverage or historical dated FX claim. No business DB, addon/source, service or external payment changes.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_processing_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-account-get"></a>

## analytic.account.get — 获取分析账户详情

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_account_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.plan, account.analytic.line, res.partner, res.currency；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.line:read, res.partner:read, res.currency:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.account.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.account.get.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.account.get --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "analytic_account_id": 1
  }
}
```

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_account_invoice_usage_inspect`。

- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.account, account.move, account.move.line；向导：无。

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：request_schema_validation
- `execute`：fixed_native_analytic_compute_or_rule_resolution
- `verify`：closed_typed_result_and_query_scope_validation
- `idempotency`：read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Closed request normalization and schemas, public CLI confirmation and deterministic keys, typed company/query/result binding, malformed input and unavailable/result identity rejection, and no fabricated delete replay. Actual native methods and ACL/constraint behavior are verified by the shared integration workflow, not by these port-level unit fixtures.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_processing_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；One shared rollback-only public CLI/real-ORM workflow passed both isolated aliases as uid 5/su=False/company 1: eight new IDs, ten setup IDs and sixteen immediate replays per alias. Native date-scoped debit/credit/balance is exact, archived/shared reads work, foreign-company shared-account lines are excluded and dated computation does not pollute all-time cache. Native posted usage counts distinct move IDs: two matching lines on one customer invoice count as one invoice, plus one vendor bill; drafts contribute zero, reflecting analytic.mixin SELECT DISTINCT move_id/account_id. Native copies preserve code/partner/active/plan/company, do not copy lines, retain the source, support serial replay/profile conflict/ambiguous-name rejection and recreate deleted targets with fresh archived identities. Native analytic-line account FK deletion denial is checked directly and preserves source/lines; unused owned accounts delete with no missing-target replay. Native applicability prefix/product-category scoring and distribution prefix/partner/tags/product/category precedence use actual references; deleting company-owned rules/models changes resolution, including native shared fallback. Distribution resolution binds audit metadata to its company singleton, not a fictitious distribution-model ID. All four writes reject shared/foreign targets; missing references are denied. Posted entries and existing plans are unchanged. Fresh-cursor synthetic data and temporary manager/analytic group rollback verified. Existing project root used, no plan mutation or dynamic DDL; no multi-root fixture coverage or historical dated FX claim. No business DB, addon/source, service or external payment changes.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_processing_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。

<a id="cap-analytic-account-search"></a>

## analytic.account.search — 搜索分析账户

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_account_search`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.plan, account.analytic.line, res.partner, res.currency；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.line:read, res.partner:read, res.currency:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.account.search.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.account.search.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.account.search --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {}
}
```

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| parameters | object | 必填 |  |  | {"additionalProperties":false,"resolved_ref":"#/$defs/parameters"} |
| parameters.query | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 搜索文本 | {"default":null,"maxLength":200} |
| parameters.active | boolean/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"default":null} |
| parameters.plan_id | integer/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"default":null,"minimum":1} |
| parameters.limit | integer | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 每页数量 | {"default":100,"maximum":1000,"minimum":1} |

<a id="cap-analytic-account-update"></a>

## analytic.account.update — 更新分析账户

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.plan, res.partner；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：res.partner:read, account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.account:write。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.account.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.account.update.response.schema.json`。

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.account.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "analytic.account.update:1:663c40c5fc625d44d00a597ae77ce292" --confirm "analytic.account.update"
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_accounting_core_write_action_as_configured_business_user
- `verify`：post_write_same_transaction_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：deterministic_request_key_and_current_target_state_recheck
- `reverse`：analytic.account.update_with_prior_values

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed public contract, fixed runtime execution, deterministic idempotency, visible marker behavior, registry schemas, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_cli.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded live smoke verifies execution, immediate replay, and rollback in both dedicated isolated database aliases.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_write_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-applicability-create"></a>

## analytic.applicability.create — 创建分析适用性规则

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`concurrent_idempotency_limit` — The handler replays the normalized selector tuple, but Odoo has no matching database uniqueness constraint for concurrent exactly-once creation.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.applicability, account.analytic.plan, product.category；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, product；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.applicability:read, account.analytic.applicability:create, account.analytic.plan:read, product.category:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.applicability.create.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.applicability.create.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.applicability.create --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "analytic.applicability.create:1:9371dff9010766d754539d04d174fa7a" --confirm "analytic.applicability.create"
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_analytic_applicability_create
- `verify`：same_transaction_record_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：company_and_full_selector_natural_key_recheck_without_concurrent_exactly_once_guarantee
- `reverse`：not_exposed_in_this_batch

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Focused unit tests cover the closed contract, root-plan and company validation, replay, runtime dispatch, schemas, and registry metadata.；引用：tests/unit/test_core_writes.py, tests/unit/test_accounting_rules_fiscal_year_contracts.py, tests/unit/test_core_writes_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_accounting_rules_fiscal_year_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The guarded shared transactional smoke passed on both dedicated isolated database aliases with first execution, immediate replay, API/ORM readback, and transaction/temporary-group rollback verification.；引用：tests/integration/test_accounting_rules_fiscal_year_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples are deferred until the capability library reaches target coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing is outside this capability batch.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-applicability-delete"></a>

## analytic.applicability.delete — 删除公司分析适用性规则

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability depends on configured database, company and business-user ACLs.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：account.analytic.applicability, res.company；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, mail；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：account.analytic.applicability:read, account.analytic.applicability:unlink, res.company:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.applicability.delete.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.applicability.delete.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.applicability.delete --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "analytic.applicability.delete:1:9bf7d7abdc771694cbe1ac2ac21deb06" --confirm "analytic.applicability.delete"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "applicability_id": 1
  }
}
```

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| parameters.applicability_id | integer | 必填（所在对象出现时） |  |  | {"minimum":1} |

| response.data.result.partial_reconcile_ids[] | integer | 每个数组元素 | allOf[3]/then |  | {"minimum":1} |
| response.data.result.full_reconcile_id | integer/null | 必填（所在对象出现时） | allOf[3]/then | 完整核销关系ID | {"minimum":1} |

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：native_copy_or_unlink_as_business_user_for_company_owned_targets
- `verify`：same_savepoint_identity_and_native_configuration_reread
- `idempotency`：serial_company_name_profile_replay_for_copy_and_no_missing_delete_replay
- `reverse`：backup_subject_to_native_acl_and_constraints

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Closed request normalization and schemas, public CLI confirmation and deterministic keys, typed company/query/result binding, malformed input and unavailable/result identity rejection, and no fabricated delete replay. Actual native methods and ACL/constraint behavior are verified by the shared integration workflow, not by these port-level unit fixtures.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_processing_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；One shared rollback-only public CLI/real-ORM workflow passed both isolated aliases as uid 5/su=False/company 1: eight new IDs, ten setup IDs and sixteen immediate replays per alias. Native date-scoped debit/credit/balance is exact, archived/shared reads work, foreign-company shared-account lines are excluded and dated computation does not pollute all-time cache. Native posted usage counts distinct move IDs: two matching lines on one customer invoice count as one invoice, plus one vendor bill; drafts contribute zero, reflecting analytic.mixin SELECT DISTINCT move_id/account_id. Native copies preserve code/partner/active/plan/company, do not copy lines, retain the source, support serial replay/profile conflict/ambiguous-name rejection and recreate deleted targets with fresh archived identities. Native analytic-line account FK deletion denial is checked directly and preserves source/lines; unused owned accounts delete with no missing-target replay. Native applicability prefix/product-category scoring and distribution prefix/partner/tags/product/category precedence use actual references; deleting company-owned rules/models changes resolution, including native shared fallback. Distribution resolution binds audit metadata to its company singleton, not a fictitious distribution-model ID. All four writes reject shared/foreign targets; missing references are denied. Posted entries and existing plans are unchanged. Fresh-cursor synthetic data and temporary manager/analytic group rollback verified. Existing project root used, no plan mutation or dynamic DDL; no multi-root fixture coverage or historical dated FX claim. No business DB, addon/source, service or external payment changes.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_processing_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-applicability-resolve"></a>

## analytic.applicability.resolve — 解析原生分析适用性

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_applicability_resolve`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability depends on configured database, company and business-user ACLs.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.plan, account.analytic.applicability, account.account, product.product, product.category；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.applicability:read, account.account:read, product.product:read, product.category:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.applicability.resolve.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.applicability.resolve.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.applicability.resolve --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "account_id": 1,
    "business_domain": "general",
    "plan_id": 1,
    "product_id": 1
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：request_schema_validation
- `execute`：fixed_native_analytic_compute_or_rule_resolution
- `verify`：closed_typed_result_and_query_scope_validation
- `idempotency`：read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Closed request normalization and schemas, public CLI confirmation and deterministic keys, typed company/query/result binding, malformed input and unavailable/result identity rejection, and no fabricated delete replay. Actual native methods and ACL/constraint behavior are verified by the shared integration workflow, not by these port-level unit fixtures.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_processing_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；One shared rollback-only public CLI/real-ORM workflow passed both isolated aliases as uid 5/su=False/company 1: eight new IDs, ten setup IDs and sixteen immediate replays per alias. Native date-scoped debit/credit/balance is exact, archived/shared reads work, foreign-company shared-account lines are excluded and dated computation does not pollute all-time cache. Native posted usage counts distinct move IDs: two matching lines on one customer invoice count as one invoice, plus one vendor bill; drafts contribute zero, reflecting analytic.mixin SELECT DISTINCT move_id/account_id. Native copies preserve code/partner/active/plan/company, do not copy lines, retain the source, support serial replay/profile conflict/ambiguous-name rejection and recreate deleted targets with fresh archived identities. Native analytic-line account FK deletion denial is checked directly and preserves source/lines; unused owned accounts delete with no missing-target replay. Native applicability prefix/product-category scoring and distribution prefix/partner/tags/product/category precedence use actual references; deleting company-owned rules/models changes resolution, including native shared fallback. Distribution resolution binds audit metadata to its company singleton, not a fictitious distribution-model ID. All four writes reject shared/foreign targets; missing references are denied. Posted entries and existing plans are unchanged. Fresh-cursor synthetic data and temporary manager/analytic group rollback verified. Existing project root used, no plan mutation or dynamic DDL; no multi-root fixture coverage or historical dated FX claim. No business DB, addon/source, service or external payment changes.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_processing_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-applicability-update"></a>

## analytic.applicability.update — 更新分析适用性规则

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.applicability, account.analytic.plan, product.category；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, product；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.applicability:read, account.analytic.applicability:write, account.analytic.plan:read, product.category:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.applicability.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.applicability.update.response.schema.json`。

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_analytic_applicability_update
- `verify`：same_transaction_record_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：target_state_recheck_without_operation_store
- `reverse`：update_with_previous_values

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Focused unit tests cover the closed contract, root-plan and company validation, replay, runtime dispatch, schemas, and registry metadata.；引用：tests/unit/test_core_writes.py, tests/unit/test_accounting_rules_fiscal_year_contracts.py, tests/unit/test_core_writes_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_accounting_rules_fiscal_year_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The guarded shared transactional smoke passed on both dedicated isolated database aliases with first execution, immediate replay, API/ORM readback, and transaction/temporary-group rollback verification.；引用：tests/integration/test_accounting_rules_fiscal_year_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples are deferred until the capability library reaches target coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing is outside this capability batch.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-distribution-resolve"></a>

## analytic.distribution.resolve — 解析原生自动分析分配

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_distribution_resolve`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability depends on configured database, company and business-user ACLs.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.distribution.model, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.plan, account.account, res.partner, res.partner.category, product.product, product.category；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.distribution.model:read, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.plan:read, account.account:read, res.partner:read, res.partner.category:read, product.product:read, product.category:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.distribution.resolve.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.distribution.resolve.response.schema.json`。

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.distribution.resolve --request "@request.json"
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：request_schema_validation
- `execute`：fixed_native_analytic_compute_or_rule_resolution
- `verify`：closed_typed_result_and_query_scope_validation
- `idempotency`：read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Closed request normalization and schemas, public CLI confirmation and deterministic keys, typed company/query/result binding, malformed input and unavailable/result identity rejection, and no fabricated delete replay. Actual native methods and ACL/constraint behavior are verified by the shared integration workflow, not by these port-level unit fixtures.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_processing_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；One shared rollback-only public CLI/real-ORM workflow passed both isolated aliases as uid 5/su=False/company 1: eight new IDs, ten setup IDs and sixteen immediate replays per alias. Native date-scoped debit/credit/balance is exact, archived/shared reads work, foreign-company shared-account lines are excluded and dated computation does not pollute all-time cache. Native posted usage counts distinct move IDs: two matching lines on one customer invoice count as one invoice, plus one vendor bill; drafts contribute zero, reflecting analytic.mixin SELECT DISTINCT move_id/account_id. Native copies preserve code/partner/active/plan/company, do not copy lines, retain the source, support serial replay/profile conflict/ambiguous-name rejection and recreate deleted targets with fresh archived identities. Native analytic-line account FK deletion denial is checked directly and preserves source/lines; unused owned accounts delete with no missing-target replay. Native applicability prefix/product-category scoring and distribution prefix/partner/tags/product/category precedence use actual references; deleting company-owned rules/models changes resolution, including native shared fallback. Distribution resolution binds audit metadata to its company singleton, not a fictitious distribution-model ID. All four writes reject shared/foreign targets; missing references are denied. Posted entries and existing plans are unchanged. Fresh-cursor synthetic data and temporary manager/analytic group rollback verified. Existing project root used, no plan mutation or dynamic DDL; no multi-root fixture coverage or historical dated FX claim. No business DB, addon/source, service or external payment changes.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_processing_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-distribution_model-create"></a>

## analytic.distribution_model.create — 创建分析分配模型

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`concurrent_idempotency_limit` — The handler scans matching selector candidates and replays one exact full target, but Odoo has no matching database uniqueness constraint for concurrent exactly-once creation.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.distribution.model, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.plan, res.partner, res.partner.category, product.product, product.category；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, product；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.distribution.model:read, account.analytic.distribution.model:create, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.plan:read, res.partner:read, res.partner.category:read, product.product:read, product.category:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.distribution_model.create.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.distribution_model.create.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.distribution_model.create --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "analytic.distribution_model.create:1:e8c11a00043eaa07c9d5db33a0ffceb7" --confirm "analytic.distribution_model.create"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",

}
```

<a id="cap-analytic-distribution_model-delete"></a>

## analytic.distribution_model.delete — 删除公司分析分配模型

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability depends on configured database, company and business-user ACLs.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：account.analytic.distribution.model, res.company；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, mail；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：account.analytic.distribution.model:read, account.analytic.distribution.model:unlink, res.company:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.distribution_model.delete.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.distribution_model.delete.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.distribution_model.delete --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "analytic.distribution_model.delete:1:f98fcc493a6f4df32478218ec3f49579" --confirm "analytic.distribution_model.delete"
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：native_copy_or_unlink_as_business_user_for_company_owned_targets
- `verify`：same_savepoint_identity_and_native_configuration_reread
- `idempotency`：serial_company_name_profile_replay_for_copy_and_no_missing_delete_replay
- `reverse`：backup_subject_to_native_acl_and_constraints

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Closed request normalization and schemas, public CLI confirmation and deterministic keys, typed company/query/result binding, malformed input and unavailable/result identity rejection, and no fabricated delete replay. Actual native methods and ACL/constraint behavior are verified by the shared integration workflow, not by these port-level unit fixtures.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_processing_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；One shared rollback-only public CLI/real-ORM workflow passed both isolated aliases as uid 5/su=False/company 1: eight new IDs, ten setup IDs and sixteen immediate replays per alias. Native date-scoped debit/credit/balance is exact, archived/shared reads work, foreign-company shared-account lines are excluded and dated computation does not pollute all-time cache. Native posted usage counts distinct move IDs: two matching lines on one customer invoice count as one invoice, plus one vendor bill; drafts contribute zero, reflecting analytic.mixin SELECT DISTINCT move_id/account_id. Native copies preserve code/partner/active/plan/company, do not copy lines, retain the source, support serial replay/profile conflict/ambiguous-name rejection and recreate deleted targets with fresh archived identities. Native analytic-line account FK deletion denial is checked directly and preserves source/lines; unused owned accounts delete with no missing-target replay. Native applicability prefix/product-category scoring and distribution prefix/partner/tags/product/category precedence use actual references; deleting company-owned rules/models changes resolution, including native shared fallback. Distribution resolution binds audit metadata to its company singleton, not a fictitious distribution-model ID. All four writes reject shared/foreign targets; missing references are denied. Posted entries and existing plans are unchanged. Fresh-cursor synthetic data and temporary manager/analytic group rollback verified. Existing project root used, no plan mutation or dynamic DDL; no multi-root fixture coverage or historical dated FX claim. No business DB, addon/source, service or external payment changes.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_processing_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-distribution_model-get"></a>

## analytic.distribution_model.get — 获取分析分摊模型详情

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_distribution_model_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.distribution.model, account.analytic.account, res.partner, res.partner.category, product.product, product.category；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, product；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.distribution.model:read, account.analytic.account:read, res.partner:read, res.partner.category:read, product.product:read, product.category:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.distribution_model.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.distribution_model.get.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.distribution_model.get --request "@request.json"
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_core_object_read_action
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_result_cursor_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed request and response, fixed bridge action, company-relative scope, ACL gates, ORM normalization, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_core_object_reads.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_bridge.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_read_cli.py, tests/unit/test_capability_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared live read-only smoke verifies the capability against both dedicated isolated database aliases as the ordinary accounting user.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_read_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-distribution_model-list"></a>

## analytic.distribution_model.list — 列出分析分摊模型

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_distribution_model_list`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.distribution.model, account.analytic.account, res.partner, res.partner.category, product.product, product.category；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, product；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.distribution.model:read, account.analytic.account:read, res.partner:read, res.partner.category:read, product.product:read, product.category:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.distribution_model.list.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.distribution_model.list.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.distribution_model.list --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {}
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_core_object_read_action
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_result_cursor_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed request and response, fixed bridge action, company-relative scope, ACL gates, ORM normalization, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_core_object_reads.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_bridge.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_read_cli.py, tests/unit/test_capability_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared live read-only smoke verifies the capability against both dedicated isolated database aliases as the ordinary accounting user.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_read_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-distribution_model-update"></a>

## analytic.distribution_model.update — 更新分析分配模型

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.distribution.model, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.plan, res.partner, res.partner.category, product.product, product.category；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, product；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.distribution.model:read, account.analytic.distribution.model:write, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.plan:read, res.partner:read, res.partner.category:read, product.product:read, product.category:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.distribution_model.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.distribution_model.update.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.distribution_model.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "analytic.distribution_model.update:1:603ff4b70ceeeda2a87631465b1a7089" --confirm "analytic.distribution_model.update"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "id": 1,
    "changes": {
      "sequence": 1
    }
  }
}
```

<a id="cap-analytic-line-create"></a>

## analytic.line.create — 创建手工分析分录

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`odoo_native_analytic_line_idempotency_field_unavailable` — The command is implemented, but Odoo has no native unique analytic-line idempotency field; V4 uses a visible deterministic name marker and cannot promise concurrent exactly-once creation.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.plan, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.line；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.line:read, account.analytic.line:create。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.line.create.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.line.create.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.line.create --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "doc-example-operation-001" --confirm "analytic.line.create"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "name": "Example",
    "date": "2026-10-31",
    "amount": "1",
    "analytic_account_id": 1
  }
}
```

<a id="cap-analytic-line-delete"></a>

## analytic.line.delete — 删除手工分析分录

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`deleted_record_tombstone_unavailable` — The handler verifies record absence but keeps no persistent tombstone, so a later retry cannot distinguish its prior deletion from an unrelated disappearance.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.line, account.analytic.account；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：account.analytic.line:read, account.analytic.line:unlink, account.analytic.account:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.line.delete.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.line.delete.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.line.delete --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "analytic.line.delete:1" --confirm "analytic.line.delete"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "analytic_line_id": 1
  }
}
```

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_manual_analytic_line_delete
- `verify`：same_transaction_record_absence_recheck_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：record_absence_recheck_without_persistent_tombstone
- `reverse`：recreate_the_manual_analytic_line

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Focused unit tests cover the closed identifier contract, manual-line restriction, company scope, absence verification, registry metadata, and schemas.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_cli.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded transactional smoke verifies execution, record absence, and rollback in both dedicated isolated database aliases.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_write_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples are deferred until the capability library reaches target coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing is outside this capability batch.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

## analytic.line.get — 获取分析分录详情

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_line_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_operations`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.line, account.analytic.account, res.partner, res.currency, product.product, uom.uom, account.account, account.move.line；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, product, uom；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.line:read, account.analytic.account:read, res.partner:read, res.currency:read, product.product:read, uom.uom:read, account.account:read, account.move.line:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.line.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.line.get.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.line.get --request "@request.json"
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_core_object_read_action
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_result_cursor_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed request and response, fixed bridge action, company scope, ACL gates, ORM normalization, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_core_object_reads.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_bridge.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_read_cli.py, tests/unit/test_capability_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared live read-only smoke verifies the capability against both dedicated isolated database aliases as the ordinary accounting user.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_read_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-line-search"></a>

## analytic.line.search — 搜索分析分录

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_line_search`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_operations`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.line, account.analytic.account, res.partner, res.currency, product.product, uom.uom, account.account, account.move.line；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, base, product, uom；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.line:read, account.analytic.account:read, res.partner:read, res.currency:read, product.product:read, uom.uom:read, account.account:read, account.move.line:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.line.search.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.line.search.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.line.search --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {}
}
```

### 请求参数（公共context与信封见总指南）

| 字段路径 | 类型 | 必填性/作用域 | 分支 | 含义 | 约束/默认值 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| parameters | object | 必填 |  |  | {"additionalProperties":false,"resolved_ref":"#/$defs/parameters"} |
| parameters.query | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 搜索文本 | {"default":null,"maxLength":200} |
| parameters.date_from | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 开始日期 | {"default":null,"format":"date"} |
| parameters.date_to | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 结束日期 | {"default":null,"format":"date"} |
| parameters.analytic_account_id | integer/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"default":null,"minimum":1} |
| parameters.limit | integer | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 每页数量 | {"default":100,"maximum":1000,"minimum":1} |
| parameters.cursor | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 不透明分页游标；新查询先省略，后续原样使用返回值 | {"default":null,"maxLength":4096,"minLength":1} |

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_core_object_read_action
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_result_cursor_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed request and response, fixed bridge action, company scope, ACL gates, ORM normalization, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_core_object_reads.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_bridge.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_read_cli.py, tests/unit/test_capability_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared live read-only smoke verifies the capability against both dedicated isolated database aliases as the ordinary accounting user.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_read_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-line-summary"></a>

## analytic.line.summary — 按分析账户汇总分析分录

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_line_summary`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：res.company, res.currency, account.analytic.plan, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.line；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, res.currency:read, account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.line:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.line.summary.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.line.summary.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.line.summary --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "date_from": "2026-10-01",
    "date_to": "2026-10-31",
    "plan_id": 1
  }
}
```

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_plan_and_date_scoped_analytic_line_summary
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_aggregate_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Focused unit tests cover the closed date, plan, and optional-account contract, fixed grouping, registry metadata, and schemas.；引用：tests/unit/test_journal_analysis.py, tests/unit/test_journal_analysis_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_return_journal_analysis_cli.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded transactional smoke verifies the fixed aggregate in both dedicated isolated database aliases.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_write_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples are deferred until the capability library reaches target coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing is outside this capability batch.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-line-update"></a>

## analytic.line.update — 更新手工分析分录

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.plan, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.line；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.line:read, account.analytic.line:write。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.line.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.line.update.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.line.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "analytic.line.update:1:663c40c5fc625d44d00a597ae77ce292" --confirm "analytic.line.update"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "analytic_line_id": 1,
    "changes": {
      "name": "Example"
    }
  }
}
```

## analytic.plan.create — 创建分析子计划

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`database_global_record_scope` — Analytic plans are database-global rather than company-owned; creating a subplan can affect other companies in the same isolated database.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.plan；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.plan:create。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.plan.create.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.plan.create.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.plan.create --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "doc-example-operation-001" --confirm "analytic.plan.create"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
    "language": "zh_CN",

}
```

## analytic.plan.get — 获取分析计划详情

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_plan_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.plan；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.plan:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.plan.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.plan.get.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.plan.get --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "plan_id": 1
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_core_object_read_action
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_result_cursor_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed request and response, fixed bridge action, ACL gates, ORM normalization, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_core_object_reads.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_bridge.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_read_cli.py, tests/unit/test_capability_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared live read-only smoke verifies the capability against both dedicated isolated database aliases as the ordinary accounting user.；引用：tests/integration/test_reference_object_read_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-analytic-plan-list"></a>

## analytic.plan.list — 列出分析计划

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`analytic_plan_list`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`accounting_master_data`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.plan；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.analytic.plan:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.plan.list.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.plan.list.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read analytic.plan.list --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {}
}
```

<a id="cap-analytic-plan-update"></a>

## analytic.plan.update — 更新分析子计划

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`database_global_record_scope` — Analytic plans are database-global rather than company-owned; updating a subplan can affect other companies in the same isolated database.
- 内部domain：`accounting_configuration`；来源模型：res.company, account.analytic.plan；向导：无。
- 必需模块：analytic, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.plan:write。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/analytic.plan.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/analytic.plan.update.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run analytic.plan.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "analytic.plan.update:1:663c40c5fc625d44d00a597ae77ce292" --confirm "analytic.plan.update"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

## budget.cancel — 取消预算

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`budgeting`；来源模型：res.company, budget.analytic, budget.line；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_budget, account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：budget.analytic:read, budget.analytic:write, budget.line:read。

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_accounting_core_write_action_as_configured_business_user
- `verify`：post_write_same_transaction_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：deterministic_request_key_and_current_state_recheck
- `reverse`：budget.reset_to_draft

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed public contract, fixed runtime execution, deterministic idempotency, visible marker behavior, registry schemas, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_cli.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded live smoke verifies execution, immediate replay, and rollback in both dedicated isolated database aliases.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_write_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-budget-confirm"></a>

## budget.confirm — 确认预算

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`budgeting`；来源模型：res.company, budget.analytic, budget.line；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_budget, account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：budget.analytic:read, budget.analytic:write, budget.line:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/budget.confirm.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/budget.confirm.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run budget.confirm --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "budget.confirm:1" --confirm "budget.confirm"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "budget_id": 1
  }
}
```

<a id="cap-budget-create"></a>

## budget.create — 创建草稿预算

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`odoo_native_budget_idempotency_field_unavailable` — The command is implemented, but Odoo has no native unique budget idempotency field; V4 uses a visible deterministic name suffix and cannot promise concurrent exactly-once creation.
- 内部domain：`budgeting`；来源模型：res.company, budget.analytic, budget.line；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_budget, account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：budget.analytic:read, budget.analytic:create, budget.line:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/budget.create.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/budget.create.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run budget.create --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "doc-example-operation-001" --confirm "budget.create"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json

  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "name": "Example",
    "date_from": "2026-10-01",
    "date_to": "2026-10-31",
    "budget_type": "revenue"
  }
}
```

## budget.get — 获取预算详情

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`budget_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`budgeting`；来源模型：res.company, budget.analytic, res.users；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_budget, account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, budget.analytic:read, res.users:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/budget.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/budget.get.response.schema.json`。

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read budget.get --request "@request.json"
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_core_object_read_action
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_result_cursor_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed request and response, fixed bridge action, company-relative scope, ACL gates, ORM normalization, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_core_object_reads.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_bridge.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_read_cli.py, tests/unit/test_capability_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared live read-only smoke verifies the capability against both dedicated isolated database aliases as the ordinary accounting user.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_read_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-budget-line-get"></a>

## budget.line.get — 获取预算行详情

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`budget_line_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`budgeting`；来源模型：res.company, budget.analytic, budget.line, res.currency, account.analytic.plan, account.analytic.account；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_budget, account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, budget.analytic:read, budget.line:read, res.currency:read, account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.account:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/budget.line.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/budget.line.get.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read budget.line.get --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "budget_line_id": 1
  }
}
```
