- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_core_object_read_action
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_result_cursor_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed request and response, fixed bridge action, company-relative scope, ACL gates, ORM normalization, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_core_object_reads.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_bridge.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_reads_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_core_object_read_cli.py, tests/unit/test_capability_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared live read-only smoke verifies the capability against both dedicated isolated database aliases as the ordinary accounting user.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_read_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-budget-line-list"></a>

## budget.line.list — 列出预算行

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`budget_line_list`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`budgeting`；来源模型：res.company, budget.analytic, budget.line, res.currency, account.analytic.plan, account.analytic.account；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_budget, account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, budget.analytic:read, budget.line:read, res.currency:read, account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.account:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/budget.line.list.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/budget.line.list.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read budget.line.list --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "budget_id": 1
  }
}
```

## budget.lines.replace — 替换预算行

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`budgeting`；来源模型：res.company, budget.analytic, budget.line, account.analytic.plan, account.analytic.account；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_budget, account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：budget.analytic:read, budget.line:read, budget.line:create, budget.line:unlink, account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.account:read。

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run budget.lines.replace --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "budget.lines.replace:1:8d47b62eded7ca535ef2c8e904813506" --confirm "budget.lines.replace"
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_accounting_core_write_action_as_configured_business_user
- `verify`：post_write_same_transaction_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：deterministic_request_key_and_current_state_recheck
- `reverse`：budget.reset_to_draft

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed public contract, fixed runtime execution, deterministic idempotency, visible marker behavior, registry schemas, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_cli.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded live smoke verifies execution, immediate replay, and rollback in both dedicated isolated database aliases.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_write_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-budget-reset_to_draft"></a>

## budget.reset_to_draft — 将预算重置为草稿

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`budgeting`；来源模型：res.company, budget.analytic, budget.line；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_budget, account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：budget.analytic:read, budget.analytic:write, budget.line:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/budget.reset_to_draft.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/budget.reset_to_draft.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run budget.reset_to_draft --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "budget.reset_to_draft:1" --confirm "budget.reset_to_draft"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "budget_id": 1
  }
}
```

<a id="cap-budget-search"></a>

## budget.search — 搜索预算

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`budget_search`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`budgeting`；来源模型：res.company, budget.analytic, res.users；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_budget, account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, budget.analytic:read, res.users:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/budget.search.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/budget.search.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read budget.search --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {}
}
```

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| parameters | object | 必填 |  |  | {"additionalProperties":false,"resolved_ref":"#/$defs/parameters"} |
| parameters.query | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 搜索文本 | {"default":null,"maxLength":200} |
| parameters.state | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 状态 | {"default":null,"enum":["draft","confirmed","revised","done","canceled",null]} |
| parameters.budget_type | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"default":null,"enum":["revenue","expense","both",null]} |
| parameters.date_from | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 开始日期 | {"default":null,"format":"date"} |
| parameters.date_to | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 结束日期 | {"default":null,"format":"date"} |
| parameters.limit | integer | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 每页数量 | {"default":100,"maximum":1000,"minimum":1} |
| parameters.cursor | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 不透明分页游标；新查询先省略，后续原样使用返回值 | {"default":null,"maxLength":4096,"minLength":1} |

<a id="cap-budget-update_draft"></a>

## budget.update_draft — 更新草稿预算

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`budgeting`；来源模型：res.company, budget.analytic, budget.line；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_budget, account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：budget.analytic:read, budget.analytic:write, budget.line:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/budget.update_draft.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/budget.update_draft.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run budget.update_draft --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "budget.update_draft:1:663c40c5fc625d44d00a597ae77ce292" --confirm "budget.update_draft"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "budget_id": 1,
    "changes": {
      "name": "Example"
    }
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_accounting_core_write_action_as_configured_business_user
- `verify`：post_write_same_transaction_reread_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：deterministic_request_key_and_current_target_state_recheck
- `reverse`：budget.update_draft_with_prior_values

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed public contract, fixed runtime execution, deterministic idempotency, visible marker behavior, registry schemas, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_writes_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_cli.py, tests/unit/test_analytic_budget_write_registry.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded live smoke verifies execution, immediate replay, and rollback in both dedicated isolated database aliases.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_write_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

## invoice.analysis.search — 搜索发票分析明细

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`invoice_analysis_search`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`invoices_and_bills`；来源模型：res.company, account.invoice.report；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.invoice.report:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/invoice.analysis.search.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/invoice.analysis.search.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read invoice.analysis.search --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {}
}
```

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_invoice_analysis_search
- `verify`：same_transaction_acl_result_cursor_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

- `integration`：`implemented`；Shared native business-line search and bulk deletion smoke passed; full rollback.；引用：tests/integration/test_management_reporting_period_batch_live.py, tests/integration/test_document_search_bulk_lines_live.py, tests/integration/test_business_line_search_remove_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-invoice-analysis-summary"></a>

## invoice.analysis.summary — 汇总发票分析

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`invoice_analysis_summary`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`invoices_and_bills`；来源模型：res.company, account.invoice.report；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.invoice.report:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/invoice.analysis.summary.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/invoice.analysis.summary.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read invoice.analysis.summary --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "date_from": "2026-10-01",
    "date_to": "2026-10-31",
    "group_by": "move_type"
  }
}
```

<a id="cap-journal_item-analysis-summary"></a>

## journal_item.analysis.summary — 汇总期间分录行活动

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`journal_item_analysis_summary`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability depends on the selected database, company, user, module, and ACLs.
- 内部domain：`general_ledger`；来源模型：account.move.line, account.account, account.journal, res.company, res.currency；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：account.move.line:read, account.account:read, account.journal:read, res.company:read, res.currency:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/journal_item.analysis.summary.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/journal_item.analysis.summary.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式


### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_posted_company_date_range_read_group
- `verify`：read_only_transaction_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the account-or-journal grouping enum, posted/date/company domain, typed totals, schema, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_journal_analysis.py, tests/unit/test_journal_analysis_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_return_journal_analysis_cli.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared guarded smoke verifies native posted journal-item aggregates in both isolated databases.；引用：tests/integration/test_return_journal_analysis_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-journal_item-analytic_distribution-replace"></a>

## journal_item.analytic_distribution.replace — 替换分录行分析分配

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Fixed line-targeted operations are implemented; native accounting, reference and analytic synchronization ACLs apply. Runtime context and native acceptance are not an unrestricted permission claim.
- 内部domain：`journal_item`；来源模型：account.account, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.line, account.analytic.plan, account.move, account.move.line, res.company；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, product, uom；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_user；ACL：account.account:read, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.line:create, account.analytic.line:read, account.analytic.line:unlink, account.analytic.plan:read, account.move.line:read, account.move.line:write, account.move:read, res.company:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/journal_item.analytic_distribution.replace.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/journal_item.analytic_distribution.replace.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run journal_item.analytic_distribution.replace --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "journal_item.analytic_distribution.replace:1:85d8621e7019aa8903e2b5935db73a39" --confirm "journal_item.analytic_distribution.replace"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "move_id": 1,
    "line_id": 1,
    "analytic_distribution": {
      "1": "100"
    }
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：native_line_or_atomic_parent_write_with_closed_existing_targets_as_business_user
- `verify`：same_transaction_parent_line_readback_with_native_constraints_and_recomputation
- `idempotency`：serial_current_target_recheck_without_operation_store_or_concurrent_exactly_once
- `reverse`：restore_prior_values_subject_to_native_constraints

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Closed typed parameters, bound parent/line results, shared CLI/schemas/confirmation/keys and target-bound pagination; native behavior is covered separately.；引用：tests/unit/test_journal_item_processing_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；One shared public CLI/real-ORM workflow passed both isolated aliases in21.41s as uid5/su=False/company1: bound line reads, target analytic keyset pages and actual partial/full reconciliation; native atomic draft entry updates preserve IDs and reject imbalance; posted maturity/full analytic replacement/clearing preserve financial fields and replay avoids child recreation; draft product UOM reprices/retaxes natively and purchase deductibility50/0/100 synchronizes native rows. Temporary analytic permission and an admin-created purchase-journal default-account fixture are transaction-local; missing/foreign parent and parent-line mismatch, posted financial writes and draft sales partial-deductibility are rejected. All objects/journal/mail alias and exact groups roll back in fresh cursors. First numeric-conversion and second analytic-sign failures retained, not acceptance; no concurrent exactly-once, receipt, business DB or real external-send claim.；引用：tests/integration/test_journal_item_processing_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples are deferred until the capability library reaches target coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing is outside this capability batch.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-journal_item-analytic_lines-list"></a>

## journal_item.analytic_lines.list — 列出指定分录行的分析分录

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`journal_item_analytic_lines_list`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Fixed line-targeted operations are implemented; native accounting, reference and analytic synchronization ACLs apply. Runtime context and native acceptance are not an unrestricted permission claim.
- 内部domain：`analytic_accounting`；来源模型：res.company, account.move.line, account.move, account.analytic.line, account.analytic.account, account.analytic.plan, res.partner, res.currency, product.product, uom.uom, account.account；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, analytic, product, uom；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.move.line:read, account.move:read, account.analytic.line:read, account.analytic.account:read, account.analytic.plan:read, res.partner:read, res.currency:read, product.product:read, uom.uom:read, account.account:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/journal_item.analytic_lines.list.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/journal_item.analytic_lines.list.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read journal_item.analytic_lines.list --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "journal_item_id": 1
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：request_schema_validation
- `execute`：fixed_company_line_target_read_as_business_user
- `verify`：typed_target_binding_and_native_keyset_cursor
- `idempotency`：read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Closed typed parameters, bound parent/line results, shared CLI/schemas/confirmation/keys and target-bound pagination; native behavior is covered separately.；引用：tests/unit/test_journal_item_processing_batch.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；One shared public CLI/real-ORM workflow passed both isolated aliases in21.41s as uid5/su=False/company1: bound line reads, target analytic keyset pages and actual partial/full reconciliation; native atomic draft entry updates preserve IDs and reject imbalance; posted maturity/full analytic replacement/clearing preserve financial fields and replay avoids child recreation; draft product UOM reprices/retaxes natively and purchase deductibility50/0/100 synchronizes native rows. Temporary analytic permission and an admin-created purchase-journal default-account fixture are transaction-local; missing/foreign parent and parent-line mismatch, posted financial writes and draft sales partial-deductibility are rejected. All objects/journal/mail alias and exact groups roll back in fresh cursors. First numeric-conversion and second analytic-sign failures retained, not acceptance; no concurrent exactly-once, receipt, business DB or real external-send claim.；引用：tests/integration/test_journal_item_processing_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-report-budget"></a>

## report.budget — 生成预算报告

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`budget_report`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`budgeting`；来源模型：res.company, budget.report, budget.analytic, budget.line, account.analytic.line, account.analytic.plan, account.analytic.account, res.users；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_budget, account, analytic, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, budget.report:read, budget.analytic:read, budget.line:read, account.analytic.line:read, account.analytic.plan:read, account.analytic.account:read, res.users:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/report.budget.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/report.budget.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read report.budget --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "budget_id": 1
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：not_applicable_read_only
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_budget_report_read_action
- `verify`：raw_budget_rows_composite_cursor_acl_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：not_applicable_read_only
- `reverse`：not_applicable_read_only

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Unit tests cover the closed report request and response, fixed bridge action, composite cursor, company and ACL scope, raw Odoo row normalization, and CLI dispatch.；引用：tests/unit/test_budget_report.py, tests/unit/test_budget_report_bridge.py, tests/unit/test_budget_report_runtime.py, tests/unit/test_budget_report_cli.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared live read-only smoke verifies the capability against both dedicated isolated database aliases as the ordinary accounting user.；引用：tests/integration/test_analytic_budget_read_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；The golden definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；The e2e definition is frozen; implementation evidence is pending.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-report-budget_account_period-set_total"></a>

## report.budget_account_period.set_total — 设置科目期间预算总额并按月分配

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user.
- 内部domain：`financial_reports`；来源模型：account.account, account.report.budget, account.report.budget.item, res.company；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, account_reports, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：account.account:read, account.report.budget.item:create, account.report.budget.item:read, account.report.budget.item:write, account.report.budget:read, account.report.budget:write。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/report.budget_account_period.set_total.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/report.budget_account_period.set_total.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run report.budget_account_period.set_total --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "report.budget_account_period.set_total:1:a9a6ec2aa44cbf468440e248540adcec" --confirm "report.budget_account_period.set_total"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "budget_definition_id": 1,
    "account_id": 1,
    "date_from": "2026-10-01",
    "date_to": "2026-10-31",
    "total": "1",
    "rounding": 1
  }
}
```

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_native_report_budget_set_total
- `verify`：same_transaction_native_record_payload_or_absence_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：requested_target_state_recheck_before_native_write
- `reverse`：repeat_native_update_with_previous_configuration

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Shared batch unit tests cover closed contracts, CLI dispatch, company/group and ACL gates, deterministic replay keys, result binding, and schemas; native workflows require the shared live smoke.；引用：tests/unit/test_report_budget_writes.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The guarded shared smoke passed both isolated aliases through the public CLI as uid 5 with su=False, exercising all eight report-budget writes, two readbacks, six immediate replays, company isolation, native monthly allocation/copy/cascade deletion, and fresh-cursor business-data and temporary-group rollback verification.；引用：tests/integration/test_report_budget_write_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples follow capability coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing follows capability coverage.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-report-budget_definition-create"></a>

## report.budget_definition.create — 创建财务报表预算

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`natural_key_replay_attribution_limit` — Natural name/account-date lookup may adopt an existing matching record; no persistent operation attribution or database uniqueness guarantees concurrent exactly-once creation.
- 内部domain：`financial_reports`；来源模型：account.report.budget, account.report.budget.item, res.company；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, account_reports, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：account.report.budget.item:read, account.report.budget:create, account.report.budget:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/report.budget_definition.create.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/report.budget_definition.create.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run report.budget_definition.create --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "report.budget_definition.create:1:4ac44a2ddc6796c59ff4d8a519ea318a" --confirm "report.budget_definition.create"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "name": "Example"
  }
}
```

- `reverse`：repeat_native_update_with_previous_configuration

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Shared batch unit tests cover closed contracts, CLI dispatch, company/group and ACL gates, deterministic replay keys, result binding, and schemas; native workflows require the shared live smoke.；引用：tests/unit/test_report_budget_writes.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The guarded shared smoke passed both isolated aliases through the public CLI as uid 5 with su=False, exercising all eight report-budget writes, two readbacks, six immediate replays, company isolation, native monthly allocation/copy/cascade deletion, and fresh-cursor business-data and temporary-group rollback verification.；引用：tests/integration/test_report_budget_write_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples follow capability coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing follows capability coverage.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

## report.budget_definition.get — 读取财务报表预算定义

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`report_budget_definition_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`financial_reports`；来源模型：res.company, account.report.budget, account.report.budget.item；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_reports, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.report.budget:read, account.report.budget.item:read。

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read report.budget_definition.get --request "@request.json"
```

- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`financial_reports`；来源模型：res.company, account.report.budget, account.report.budget.item；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_reports, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.report.budget:read, account.report.budget.item:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/report.budget_definition.search.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/report.budget_definition.search.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read report.budget_definition.search --request "@request.json"
```

> 合成示例仅验证请求Schema；不证明记录存在、权限/配置满足或业务执行成功。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {}
}
```

### 请求参数（公共context与信封见总指南）

| 字段路径 | 类型 | 必填性/作用域 | 分支 | 含义 | 约束/默认值 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| parameters | object | 必填 |  |  | {"additionalProperties":false,"resolved_ref":"#/$defs/parameters"} |
| parameters.query | string/null | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 搜索文本 | {"default":null,"maxLength":200,"minLength":1,"pattern":"^(?:\\S&#124;\\S[\\s\\S]*\\S)$"} |

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；The focused test covers registry metadata, fixed CLI routing, model mapping, and report audit IDs.；引用：tests/unit/test_accounting_supporting_object_reads_registry_cli.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The shared uid-5 read-only smoke passed on both isolated databases, verifying model/field availability, ACL, empty pages, and missing-record errors; populated-row and computed-field live evidence remains pending.；引用：tests/integration/test_accounting_supporting_object_reads_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples are deferred until target coverage.；引用：无

<a id="cap-report-budget_definition-update"></a>

## report.budget_definition.update — 更新财务报表预算

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user.
- 内部domain：`financial_reports`；来源模型：account.report.budget, account.report.budget.item, res.company；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, account_reports, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：account.report.budget.item:read, account.report.budget:read, account.report.budget:write。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/report.budget_definition.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/report.budget_definition.update.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run report.budget_definition.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "report.budget_definition.update:1:f15d0170a3b8cb6ef2b71cbfc6979d02" --confirm "report.budget_definition.update"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "budget_definition_id": 1,
    "changes": {
      "name": "Example"
    }
  }
}
```

<a id="cap-report-budget_item-create"></a>

## report.budget_item.create — 创建财务报表预算项

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`natural_key_replay_attribution_limit` — Natural name/account-date lookup may adopt an existing matching record; no persistent operation attribution or database uniqueness guarantees concurrent exactly-once creation.
- 内部domain：`financial_reports`；来源模型：account.account, account.report.budget, account.report.budget.item, res.company；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, account_reports, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：account.account:read, account.report.budget.item:create, account.report.budget.item:read, account.report.budget:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/report.budget_item.create.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/report.budget_item.create.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run report.budget_item.create --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "report.budget_item.create:1:9859c98eb92d3e507a558e0295bb953b" --confirm "report.budget_item.create"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

<a id="cap-report-budget_item-delete"></a>

## report.budget_item.delete — 删除财务报表预算项

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`degraded`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`deleted_record_tombstone_unavailable` — Native deletion is verified, but no persistent tombstone attributes a later missing-record retry to this operation.
- 内部domain：`financial_reports`；来源模型：account.report.budget, account.report.budget.item, res.company；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, account_reports, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：account.report.budget.item:read, account.report.budget.item:unlink, account.report.budget:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/report.budget_item.delete.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/report.budget_item.delete.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式


- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_native_report_budget_delete

- `reverse`：not_reversible_deleted_configuration_must_be_recreated

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Shared batch unit tests cover closed contracts, CLI dispatch, company/group and ACL gates, deterministic replay keys, result binding, and schemas; native workflows require the shared live smoke.；引用：tests/unit/test_report_budget_writes.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The guarded shared smoke passed both isolated aliases through the public CLI as uid 5 with su=False, exercising all eight report-budget writes, two readbacks, six immediate replays, company isolation, native monthly allocation/copy/cascade deletion, and fresh-cursor business-data and temporary-group rollback verification.；引用：tests/integration/test_report_budget_write_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples follow capability coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing follows capability coverage.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。

<a id="cap-report-budget_item-get"></a>

## report.budget_item.get — 读取财务报表预算项

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`report_budget_item_get`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`financial_reports`；来源模型：res.company, account.report.budget, account.report.budget.item, account.account；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_reports, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.report.budget:read, account.report.budget.item:read, account.account:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/report.budget_item.get.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/report.budget_item.get.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read report.budget_item.get --request "@request.json"
```

## report.budget_item.search — 搜索财务报表预算项

- 类型：只读；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`report_budget_item_search`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — Availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user at runtime.
- 内部domain：`financial_reports`；来源模型：res.company, account.report.budget, account.report.budget.item, account.account；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account_reports, account, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_readonly；ACL：res.company:read, account.report.budget:read, account.report.budget.item:read, account.account:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/report.budget_item.search.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/report.budget_item.search.response.schema.json`。

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 read report.budget_item.search --request "@request.json"
```

<a id="cap-report-budget_item-update"></a>

## report.budget_item.update — 更新财务报表预算项

- 类型：写入；静态状态：`unconfigured`；handler：`core_write`。
- 状态原因：`runtime_context_required` — The fixed handler is implemented; availability is evaluated for each configured database, company, and user.
- 内部domain：`financial_reports`；来源模型：account.account, account.report.budget, account.report.budget.item, res.company；向导：无。
- 必需模块：account, account_reports, base；配置项：database_alias, company_allowlist, user_mapping；公司条件：`required`。
- 权限组：account.group_account_manager；ACL：account.account:read, account.report.budget.item:read, account.report.budget.item:write, account.report.budget:read。
- 请求/响应合同：`schemas/v1/report.budget_item.update.request.schema.json` / `schemas/v1/report.budget_item.update.response.schema.json`。

### 调用方式

```bash
odoo-accounting-cli-v4 write run report.budget_item.update --request "@request.json" --idempotency-key "report.budget_item.update:1:3f4c1169476029ba3e4e36a4fbebfd59" --confirm "report.budget_item.update"
```

> 合成示例通过请求Schema和当前纯写入参数校验；展示键由当前代码计算，无Odoo执行。真实ID和参数变更后必须重算。

```json
{
  "schema_version": "v1",
  "request_id": "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111",
  "context": {
    "database": "v4-dev",
    "company_id": 1,
    "user_login": "example.operator",
    "language": "zh_CN",
    "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"
  },
  "parameters": {
    "budget_item_id": 1,
    "changes": {
      "account_id": 1
    }
  }
}
```

### 请求参数（公共context与信封见总指南）

| 字段路径 | 类型 | 必填性/作用域 | 分支 | 含义 | 约束/默认值 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| parameters | object | 必填 |  |  | {"additionalProperties":false,"required_in_object":["budget_item_id","changes"]} |
| parameters.budget_item_id | integer | 必填（所在对象出现时） |  |  | {"minimum":1,"resolved_ref":"report.budget_definition.create.request.schema.json#/$defs/id"} |
| parameters.changes | object | 必填（所在对象出现时） |  | 仅提交拟变更字段，非整条记录 | {"additionalProperties":false,"minProperties":1} |
| parameters.changes.account_id | integer | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 会计科目ID | {"minimum":1,"resolved_ref":"report.budget_definition.create.request.schema.json#/$defs/id"} |
| parameters.changes.date | string | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  |  | {"format":"date","pattern":"^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}$","resolved_ref":"report.budget_definition.create.request.schema.json#/$defs/date"} |
| parameters.changes.amount | string | 可选（可能有条件限制） |  | 十进制数值；金额、固定税额或税率按所在业务对象解释 | {"maxLength":18,"not":{"const":"-0"},"pattern":"^-?(?:0&#124;[1-9][0-9]*)(?:\\.[0-9]*[1-9])?$(?![\\s\\S])","resolved_ref":"report.budget_definition.create.request.schema.json#/$defs/amount"} |

### 执行、验证、幂等与逆向边界

- `preview`：exact_capability_confirmation_and_closed_request_validation
- `execute`：fixed_company_scoped_native_report_budget_update
- `verify`：same_transaction_native_record_payload_or_absence_and_response_schema_validation
- `idempotency`：requested_target_state_recheck_before_native_write
- `reverse`：repeat_native_update_with_previous_configuration

### 已登记测试与证据范围

- `unit`：`implemented`；Shared batch unit tests cover closed contracts, CLI dispatch, company/group and ACL gates, deterministic replay keys, result binding, and schemas; native workflows require the shared live smoke.；引用：tests/unit/test_report_budget_writes.py
- `integration`：`implemented`；The guarded shared smoke passed both isolated aliases through the public CLI as uid 5 with su=False, exercising all eight report-budget writes, two readbacks, six immediate replays, company isolation, native monthly allocation/copy/cascade deletion, and fresh-cursor business-data and temporary-group rollback verification.；引用：tests/integration/test_report_budget_write_batch_live.py
- `golden`：`planned`；Golden examples follow capability coverage.；引用：无
- `e2e`：`planned`；Natural-language routing follows capability coverage.；引用：无

登记为implemented不等于本次重新跑过，也不等于全部配置/全部接口真实E2E通过。
